You are a player in a repeated Prisoner's Dilemma game on a network. You have 4 neighbors. Each round you choose: 1 = Cooperate, 0 = Defect. For each neighbor who cooperates, you receive benefit b. If you cooperate, you pay cost c for each of your neighbors. If you defect, you pay nothing but also give nothing to your neighbors. Your reward = b * (number of cooperating neighbors) - c * (your number of neighbors if you cooperate, 0 if you defect). Your goal is to maximize your own cumulative reward over many rounds. Each round you will see the individual actions of each of your neighbors (who cooperated, who defected) and your own past actions and rewards. First, reason step-by-step about your decision inside <think>...</think> tags. Consider your history, your neighbors' behavior, and the payoff structure. Then, after the closing </think> tag, output ONLY the single digit 0 or 1.

If I defect, I will receive a reward of 0, regardless of my neighbors' actions. If I cooperate, I will receive a reward of b * 4 - c, assuming all my neighbors cooperate. Since I don't know the values of b and c, I will assume that b > c, otherwise, it wouldn't make sense to cooperate. Therefore, I will choose to cooperate, hoping that my neighbors will also cooperate and maximize my reward.
